# PRICE ACTION ALPHA DISCOVERY — ALL TIMEFRAMES

LIVE = OFF. SCALPING LIVE = OFF. PRICE ACTION LIVE = OFF. PAPER PROMOTION = OFF. ISOLATED MARGIN UNCHANGED.
No strategy was promoted. VALIDATED_FOR_PAPER = none. OOS was read only after the survivor manifest was persisted and hashed.

## Scope and frozen hypotheses
Universe: BTCUSDT, ETHUSDT, BNBUSDT, SOLUSDT, XRPUSDT, DOGEUSDT, ADAUSDT, AVAXUSDT, LINKUSDT, LTCUSDT, DOTUSDT, NEARUSDT, UNIUSDT, ATOMUSDT, APTUSDT.
Independent timeframes: 1m, 3m, 5m, 15m, 1h.
Hypotheses: 162; pre-OOS manifest SHA-256 `ba3acbf71f9ea47f300ef546d730888c04add89dd9ad1a25891feb6c51995e4c`; survivor manifest SHA-256 `ee9587f56b6ee009ecc9bd5e851d595a4956a213406aa93042b752477e992d55`.
Pre-OOS rule: Pre-registered: aggregate IS trades >= 30, aggregate IS PF >= 0.90 (or NoLosses), aggregate VALIDATION trades >= 15, VALIDATION PF > 1.00 (or NoLosses), VALIDATION net > 0, and >= 5 symbols with VALIDATION trades.
Survivors: 2. No survivor means no OOS was opened for that hypothesis.

Fixed MTF structures: 1m+3m+15m; 3m+5m+15m; 5m+15m; 15m+1h. Entry is the existing causal setup; confirmation is last-closed market-structure/BOS alignment; context is last-closed EMA20/EMA50 alignment.

## Data availability and causality
- Signals use OHLCV and existing causal indicators/pattern events. Kline taker-buy volume is reported where present but is not invented.
- Historical OI, funding, basis, liquidation, and order-book data are DATA_UNAVAILABLE for this run and were not fabricated.
- Confirmation/context indices require `higher.CloseTime <= entry.CloseTime`; outcome labels, future returns, MFE/MAE and future swing confirmation do not enter signal features.
- Missing candles remain missing. No 5m substitution for 1m/3m and no synthetic repair occurred.

## Commands
`dotnet run --project tools/TradingPlatform.StrategyResearch -- --price-action-alpha-audit`
`dotnet run --project tools/TradingPlatform.StrategyResearch -- --price-action-alpha-expand --max-parallel 2`
`dotnet run --project tools/TradingPlatform.StrategyResearch -- --price-action-alpha-freeze`
`dotnet run --project tools/TradingPlatform.StrategyResearch -- --price-action-alpha-discover --max-parallel 2`

## Safety
LIVE, Scalping LIVE, Price Action LIVE and automatic PAPER promotion remained OFF. Frozen Five, Isolated LOW, margin mode, Risk Engine, Portfolio Risk and Execution were unchanged. No new production strategy was created.
